Stock spike desk - 210 F&O stocks, 84 sessions 2026-06-01 .. 2026-09-28. First 5 sessions are history only; IN 44 sessions to 2026-08-10, OUT 35 sessions.
Cash MIS: capital Rs 10,672.74, 5x, 6% risk cap with costs inside, cost <= 20% of risk, target >= 1R, net at target >= Rs 100; one position; 15% daily halt; entries 09:30-14:59; square-off 15:14.
Regime gate (pace 1.3, midday 1.5) on the entry timeframe, always (applied before evaluate, as the engine does).
metric A = ATR5 / median TR of the 200 bars before; B = 15-min range / the same 15 min on prior sessions.
rv = 5-min volume / same 5 min on prior sessions (0 = off). N = top-N |move from prev close| (0 = all liquid).
entry: smcT = ob_fvg_retest on T-minute bars within W min of a spike; mom_* = at the bar a threshold is crossed.
tgt: M = the engine's momentum target (needs >= 1R); F = fixed 2R target (target_mode 'fixed').
ctrl = same entry, same minutes, on a random liquid stock that was not spiking (A < 1.5 and B < 1.5).
